# Decision ledger

Every decision made on sverify, for contributors: the reference's D-numbered
decisions ([decisions.md](decisions.md)), the theory advisor's numbered rulings
and the maintainer's rulings, one entry each, with who decided, its scope, its
status and what it replaces.

Each entry is a file in [`decisions/`](../decisions/), checked against
[`decision.schema.json`](../decisions/decision.schema.json) by
`python -m sverify_dev.decisions`, which also writes this page. A ruling's
text stays in the coordinator's notes folder, and its entry names the file.
An entry whose standing the record does not settle is `unclear`, with the
reason.

## Counts

| Record | in-force | unclear | superseded | withdrawn | reserved | total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| decisions.md | 63 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 6 | 70 |
| theory advisor's rulings | 154 | 1 | 11 | 6 | 0 | 172 |
| maintainer's rulings | 154 | 1 | 15 | 0 | 0 | 170 |

## What's in force

Each scope's entries in force, newest first. An amended entry is in force as
amended.

### Reference semantics

- [F193](#f193) (2026-11-20): D69 signed: Ibis calendar functions of a UTC instant mean that function of its UTC wall clock; the Ibis producer writes the conversion, the parser keeps refusing; SQL lift, arithmetic, other zones stay refused.
- [F191](#f191) (2026-11-20): quantile_cont/median/Ibis quantile of a decimal stay refused at 1.0 by design, message names the cast to double; quantile_disc of decimals needs no decision; no width rule in core; D68 reserved.
- [F188](#f188) (2026-11-20): Decimal % is refused where max(p1-s1, p2-s2)+max(s1,s2) > 38 (DuckDB yields DOUBLE there); D56's operand check does not apply; conditions X1-X6.
- [F187](#f187) (2026-11-20): D67 signed: opaque columns as a new Kind.opaque with no new Value; the kind checker refuses every use but carrying (and IS NULL), lowerings write plain column references, facts first.
- [F186](#f186) (2026-11-20): Binary values (#1918) and struct casts (#1919) refused by design at 1.0, list casts proceed; float32 constants read under P1-P5; boolean casts and #1934 need no decision; interval casts under T1-T4; D65 and D66 reserved. Amended by [F187](#f187).
- [F181](#f181) (2026-11-20): Decimal % accepted as D62 (truncated remainder at common scale, conditions M1-M9); unnest keep_empty=True accepted as frontend composition; unnest offset= dropped (D64 reserved); substring with length expression dropped (D63 reserved, general ternary node). Amended by [F188](#f188).
- [F177](#f177) (2026-11-20): An integer cast to DECIMAL(p>=19, 0) is read as a decimal through the general branch; the wide-int-decimal special case is deleted in Lean, Python writer and Polars together; corrected reading noticed to users. Amended by [F179](#f179).
- [F171](#f171) (2026-11-20): Corrects ruling 154's condition 1 (MAX and AVG fail htr at all three engines; refusal stands) and rules that list_null_order's scope may be pinned to list arguments in a later PR (L1 to L6), adding a row to D34's table.
- [D69](#d69) (2026-11-20): Ibis calendar functions of a UTC instant mean that function of its UTC wall clock.
- [D67](#d67) (2026-11-20): Opaque columns get a kind of their own with no value; every use but carrying them and IS NULL is refused.
- [D62](#d62) (2026-11-20): Decimal % is the truncated remainder at the operands' common scale.
- [F164](#f164) (2026-11-19): A join may evaluate a raising conjunct only on pairs that pass the other conjuncts: such pairs are in no allowed result, so by D16 the engine may return there; the split of a mixed residual is admitted. No D-number; a dated note goes under D19. Amended by [F164.A1](#f164a1), [F170](#f170).
- [F144](#f144) (2026-11-19): Addendum to ruling 139 after the default-lens decision: 139 stands; unordered collect is determined at the default, unordered string_agg is a tie place at every observation; Value.Equiv's struct arm is a defect fixed in P1; P3 becomes required.
- [F142](#f142) (2026-11-19): Signs D61: timestamps count microseconds or nanoseconds (new kinds timeNs, timeTzNs); s and ms are read as microseconds at the scan; narrowing casts floor; µs to ns raises overflow outside 64 bits; results stay timestamp(6).
- [F138](#f138) (2026-11-19): D56 (decimal +/- across scales rescales each operand to the result type and raises on overflow) is approved; approval logged as ruling 138.
- [F137](#f137) (2026-11-19): D58: timestamp delta counts unit boundaries crossed (floor index difference); D59: strict string-to-timestamp parsing with a fixed directive set that raises a domain error on mismatch. Facts first; week, nanosecond, calendar units refused for now.
- [F135](#f135) (2026-11-19): F3's corollary takes a decidable side condition `SumTolCovers n bs` (the exact inequality, not a threshold); track A later fixes the checker's subnormal sum ε term and then the condition is dropped; a fresh ε is ruled out. Amended by [F146](#f146).
- [F133](#f133) (2026-11-19): #1796 merges as is; D57 signed: the reference's double (and fp32) abs becomes IEEE, clearing the sign (abs(-0.0)=+0.0), in a facts-first follow-up PR; writers then emit native abs.
- [F119](#f119) (2026-11-19): In #1765 (D53 step 2) the Ibis frontend refuses mixes of zoned and naive timestamps in _common (added D53 sentence removed); UnFn.atUtc passes .other through as .other; K5 may stay in the PR with its own fragment.
- [F112](#f112) (2026-11-19): K5 fix accepted: Expr.constIssues reads a lambda's body, shipped as its own soundness PR ahead of D53 step 2; Ibis mixes of instants and naive timestamps stay refused, raised in the producer's _common helper. Amended by [F119](#f119).
- [F106](#f106) (2026-11-19): SQL CAST of a double to BIGINT lifts as ftoi(froundEven x); D55 assigned (fp.round accepts TIE_TO_EVEN at zero digits, default unchanged); decimals lift as rescale then cast; all tier-1 writers write froundEven; #322 does not gate 1.0.
- [F101](#f101) (2026-11-19): D53 ships in two steps: step 1 instant column values (naive literals vs instant columns refused, functions refused by default); step 2 a core conversion function at UTC (like widen) with instant literals built from it and a lowering regen.
- [F99](#f99) (2026-11-19): D53 survey: (a) and (c) accepted; the parser is the gate for instant/naive mixes, Kind.comparable and Kind.subK unchanged; conditions Z3a-Z3h (literal gate via argKind, IN, functions refused by default, CASE and set operations keep the kind) replace Z3. Amended by [F101](#f101).
- [F97](#f97) (2026-11-19): D53: a UTC-zoned timestamp is an instant (Kind.timeTz); the reference computes with it where no zone is read and refuses calendar functions, the mix with naive timestamps and other zones; written back as an instant. Options A and B not built. Amended by [F101](#f101), [F99](#f99).
- [F92](#f92) (2026-11-19): 12b-2's statements confirmed with repairs: `Around` moves to the core, comparison theorem with T1-T3, shape-recognizer rule with R1-R8. 12d-4 and 12b-2 become two PRs (S1-S3, no release cut between). D52 gains a dated `Around` amendment.
- [F89](#f89) (2026-11-19): D50 lift capped at the top N nesting levels (N=3 or 2 by measurement) as an interim; D50-d removes the cap and is a 1.0 gate; Spark coverage accepted with checks C5-C6; unproved range/pattern guards accepted, D50-e (range guards) before 1.0. Amended by [F94](#f94).
- [F86](#f86) (2026-11-19): 12b-1 is a core theorem: integer-valued double sums and means within 2^53 are exact for every sum-order behaviour; `StdModel.ExactInts` accepted as a hypothesis (not axiom), `Nearest` not needed; D35 amended to add exactness on small integers for shapeless orders.
- [F85](#f85) (2026-11-19): The 12d-4 source mark is a core identity `.un (.approx k)` in the plan, no side channel; lowerings erase it; `exprEps` reads its band. Assigns D52; `Around` is an obligation for marked integer quotients. Amended by [F197](#f197).
- [F84](#f84) (2026-11-19): #1693's first-range bound of avgBigint_near as ulpDiv of the quotient is accepted as written; the set (Around) is exact, 12b-2 rests on it, and 12d-4 reads the bound in Epsilon's own unit.
- [U-2026-11-19-rounding-cast-refusal](#u-2026-11-19-rounding-cast-refusal) (2026-11-19): User's (b): under the reference the SQL lift refuses CAST of a double or decimal to an integer, with hints, until #322 (D54). Amended by [F109](#f109).
- [D61](#d61) (2026-11-19): Timestamps count microseconds or nanoseconds; second and millisecond columns are read as microseconds, narrowing casts floor, and µs to ns raises overflow outside 64 bits.
- [D59](#d59) (2026-11-19): String-to-timestamp parsing is strict, with a fixed directive set, and raises a domain error on a mismatch.
- [D58](decisions.md#d58-a-timestamps-delta-counts-the-unit-boundaries-crossed) (2026-11-19): A timestamp's delta counts the unit boundaries crossed.
- [D57](#d57) (2026-11-19): The reference's double abs is IEEE: it clears the sign, so abs(-0.0) is +0.0.
- [D56](decisions.md#d56-decimal--and---check-each-operand-at-the-results-scale) (2026-11-19): Decimal + and − check each operand at the result's scale.
- [D55](#d55) (2026-11-19): fp.round accepts TIE_TO_EVEN at zero digits, so SQL CAST of a double to BIGINT lifts as a cast of the rounded value; the default is unchanged.
- [D54](decisions.md#d54-the-lift-refines-the-dialect) (2026-11-19): The SQL lift refines the dialect: lifted DuckDB SQL means DuckDB's value; rounding casts are refused until #322.
- [D53](decisions.md#d53-a-timestamp-with-the-time-zone-utc-is-an-instant) (2026-11-19): A timestamp with the time zone UTC is an instant. Amended by [D69](#d69), [F101](#f101), [F193](#f193).
- [D52](#d52) (2026-11-19): An integer quotient's source mark is a core identity in the plan whose band the error bound reads; lowerings erase it. Amended by [F104](#f104), [F108](#f108), [F92](#f92).
- [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48) (2026-11-18): D48: a result is determined up to row order and D31's class members; Report.deterministic reports this class level (Rel.detClass), the exact level (Rel.detPlan) applies only to observations reading members; floats='plan' compares class-blind.
- [F-1631-str-ascii-group](#f-1631-str-ascii-group) (2026-11-18): non_ascii_text sits on str.case and a new group str.ascii (reverse, ascii, levenshtein, ilike, regexp_*); str.plain and str.opaque carry no option. A check ties group membership to a nonAscii arm. No new D-number. Amended by [F-1631-regexp-match-read](#f-1631-regexp-match-read).
- [F-1608-cast-double-bigint](#f-1608-cast-double-bigint) (2026-11-18): CAST(DOUBLE AS BIGINT) under semantics='duckdb': lift casts of round/floor/ceil arguments now (step 0); the bare cast needs a half-even round in the core via a dated amendment to D42's rounding row, then the lift; B declined. Amended by [F106](#f106).
- [F-1497-decimal-div-statement](#f-1497-decimal-div-statement) (2026-11-18): sverify_decimal_div's declared type is checked by a post-pass over the parsed plan with a kinds branch and a SUM/COUNT shape branch, one inequality p >= P + k with no min 38. Amended by [F63](#f63).
- [F76](#f76) (2026-11-18): D50 lift statements in #1681 confirmed as case lemmas; required before the label: refinement theorem Expr.andLift_refines/orLift_refines, BoolVal closure, D50 tests, corrected D50 docs (copies, constants via D21), size measurements. Steps D50-b, -c, -d follow. Amended by [F77](#f77), [F89](#f89).
- [F63](#f63) (2026-11-18): sverify_decimal_div is no longer refused: where the declared type does not follow from the operands it reads as the checked decimal (Safety reports overflow); where it follows it stays dkind. Amended by [F73](#f73).
- [F62](#f62) (2026-11-18): D51 raise sets: UPPER raises on strings holding one of 157 scalars, LOWER on 56 scalars plus every Σ (57); one set per function, no context sets; Spark's writer refuses non-ASCII UPPER/LOWER until its library and locale facts exist; generated Lean/Python table. Amended by [F66](#f66), [F68](#f68), [F80](#f80).
- [U-2026-11-18-class-pick-determined](#u-2026-11-18-class-pick-determined) (2026-11-18): A place that only picks a member of such a class counts as determined when the required observation cannot tell members apart.
- [D51](decisions.md#d51-case-mapping-has-the-value-the-engines-agree-on) (2026-11-18): UPPER and LOWER map a character as all tier-1 engines do and raise where they differ. Amended by [F62](#f62), [F80](#f80).
- [D50](decisions.md#d50-the-frontends-lift-and-and-or-so-a-guard-protects-the-next-operand) (2026-11-18): The frontends lift AND and OR so a guard protects the next operand; the core stays eager. Amended by [F102](#f102), [F76](#f76), [F77](#f77), [F89](#f89), [F94](#f94).
- [D48](decisions.md#d48-a-result-is-determined-up-to-the-classes-its-observation-reads) (2026-11-18): A result is determined up to row order and the member of each class its observation does not read.
- [D47](decisions.md#d47-raise-value-or-open-where-engines-differ) (2026-11-18): Where tier-1 engines differ, the reference raises, gives a value or stays open by the first rule that applies.
- [D43](decisions.md#d43-text-the-engines-map-differently-is-an-issue-nonascii) (2026-11-18): Text the engines map differently (ILIKE, reverse, regex classes, ...) is an issue, nonAscii. Amended by [D51](decisions.md#d51-case-mapping-has-the-value-the-engines-agree-on), [F-1631-str-ascii-group](#f-1631-str-ascii-group).
- [U-2026-11-17-equivalence-class-values](#u-2026-11-17-equivalence-class-values) (2026-11-17): Values that compare equal on every engine form one class, which member is returned does not matter, and opening such a pin is pre-approved for Fable.
- [D46](decisions.md#d46-an-integer-columns-values-lie-in-its-declared-width) (2026-11-17): An integer column's values lie in its declared width; a source value outside fails the read.
- [D45](decisions.md#d45-quantile_disc-picks-a-member-of-a-class-as-min-and-max-do) (2026-11-17): QUANTILE_DISC picks a member of a class, as MIN and MAX do; window frames join. Amended by [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48).
- [D44](decisions.md#d44-replace-with-an-empty-pattern-is-the-string-unchanged) (2026-11-17): replace with an empty pattern is the string unchanged.
- [D42](decisions.md#d42-the-references-value-of-the-options-no-earlier-decision-states) (2026-11-15): Records the reference's value of every option no earlier decision states; changes no behaviour. Amended by [D55](#d55), [F-1608-cast-double-bigint](#f-1608-cast-double-bigint), [F106](#f106).
- [D41](decisions.md#d41-modulus-of-doubles-accepts-division_type-floor) (2026-11-15): modulus of doubles accepts division_type FLOOR; TRUNCATE (C's fmod) stays the default.
- [U-2026-11-09-small-guardable-rejections](#u-2026-11-09-small-guardable-rejections) (2026-11-09): The reference's hard rejections stay a small enumerable set (zero divisors, overflow, domain errors) that users can guard with preconditions.
- [U-2026-11-09-row-order-certified-not-forced](#u-2026-11-09-row-order-certified-not-forced) (2026-11-09): The reference shims only cheap value levels and certifies but never forces row order, which is determined iff the ORDER BY key is a superkey of the output.
- [U-2026-11-09-determinism-certificate](#u-2026-11-09-determinism-certificate) (2026-11-09): Determinism is a proved certificate over whichever profile a plan is verified under, and the deterministic profile is dropped, with reproducible= as the rewrite that earns it.
- [D40](decisions.md#d40-power1--is-cs-pow) (2026-11-09): power(±1, ±∞) is C's pow (1), the pow_one_inf option. Amended by [F163](#f163).
- [D39](decisions.md#d39-division-by-zero-has-an-ieee-value) (2026-11-09): on_division_by_zero gains IEEE: x / 0.0 is IEEE 754's quotient on the raw codes.
- [U-2026-11-08-substring-limit-reference-profile](#u-2026-11-08-substring-limit-reference-profile) (2026-11-08): The substring range limit belongs to the reference profile (substring_range ERROR), not to DuckDB; only the justification comments change.
- [U-2026-11-08-reference-doubles-model](#u-2026-11-08-reference-doubles-model) (2026-11-08): Reference doubles have one NaN, two infinities and one zero, and creating a NaN or overflowing from finite values is an error.
- [U-2026-11-08-core-superset-reference-subset](#u-2026-11-08-core-superset-reference-subset) (2026-11-08): The core is the abstract superset of behaviours and the reference profile picks a subset every engine has natively or reaches with a cheap writer shim.
- [U-2026-11-08-core-exact-total](#u-2026-11-08-core-exact-total) (2026-11-08): The core is exact and total on its domain, with inexact values as Allowed sets or raises and everything approximate stated in a profile.
- [U-2026-11-08-canon-at-identity-boundaries](#u-2026-11-08-canon-at-identity-boundaries) (2026-11-08): Canon applies only at identity boundaries (comparisons, DISTINCT, GROUP BY, join and window keys), while reads and scalar ops keep raw values.
- [D35](decisions.md#d35-engine-summation-orders-are-bounded-sets) (2026-11-08): Engine summation orders are bounded sets (SumOrder), bounding every behaviour but EXACT. Amended by [F86](#f86).
- [D34](decisions.md#d34-an-option-differs-from-the-reference-only-on-its-scope) (2026-11-08): An option differs from the reference only on its scope, a predicate stated beside the option. Amended by [F171](#f171).
- [D32](decisions.md#d32-calendar-functions-work-on-the-references-timestamps-0001-to-9999) (2026-11-08): Calendar functions work on the reference's timestamps, 0001-01-01 to 9999-12-31.
- [D31](decisions.md#d31-double-arithmetic-is-exact-rounded-once) (2026-11-07): Double +, −, × and ÷ are the exact result rounded once; the reference is IEEE on the full code set and canon applies only at identity boundaries. Amended by [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48), [U-2026-11-08-canon-at-identity-boundaries](#u-2026-11-08-canon-at-identity-boundaries).
- [D30](decisions.md#d30-statistical-aggregates-are-correctly-rounded-exact-moments) (2026-11-07): VAR, STDDEV, COVAR and CORR of doubles are rounded once from the exact moments.
- [D29](decisions.md#d29-a-value-has-its-kind) (2026-11-07): Value has a constructor per kind (int, float, date, ts, tod, dec, ...).
- [U-2026-11-05-ties-open-reproducible](#u-2026-11-05-ties-open-reproducible) (2026-11-05): The reference leaves tie order and tie pick open, contracts must hold for every tie choice, and a total order is needed only when reproducible= asks.
- [U-2026-11-04-tighten-reference](#u-2026-11-04-tighten-reference) (2026-11-04): Where an engine differs on an edge case, the reference raises under a precondition rather than shimming, when that barely affects practicality.
- [D38](decisions.md#d38-no-operation-on-finite-doubles-overflows-to-infinity) (2026-11-04): An operation on finite doubles whose IEEE result is ±∞ raises; ±∞ from the data passes through.
- [D28](decisions.md#d28-lambdas-have-lexical-scope) (2026-11-04): A lambda in a higher-order call has lexical scope and sees the enclosing row.
- [D27](decisions.md#d27-the-size-hypothesis-is-the-only-size-assumption) (2026-11-04): Rel.Small (string, window and aggregate sizes) is the only size assumption, a hypothesis and never an axiom.
- [D26](decisions.md#d26-two-equalities-for-null) (2026-11-04): NULL has two equalities: unknown in predicates, equal in GROUP BY, DISTINCT and set operations.
- [D25](decisions.md#d25-float-aggregates-are-correctly-rounded-exact-sums) (2026-11-04): Double SUM, AVG and the statistical aggregates are the correctly rounded value of the exact sum or moments.
- [D24](decisions.md#d24-tie-order-is-open-and-determinism-is-proved) (2026-11-04): The reference leaves tie_order and tie_pick open, and determinism is a proved property. Amended by [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48).
- [D21](decisions.md#d21-tighten-the-reference-where-it-stays-practical) (2026-11-04): Sverify tightens the reference (raise plus a rarely binding precondition) where that costs little, over engine shims. Amended by [D47](decisions.md#d47-raise-value-or-open-where-engines-differ).
- [D19](decisions.md#d19-and-and-or-raise-eagerly) (2026-11-04): AND and OR raise eagerly in the core: an error in either operand may raise. Amended by [D50](decisions.md#d50-the-frontends-lift-and-and-or-so-a-guard-protects-the-next-operand).
- [D18](decisions.md#d18-observations-ordered-by-refinement) (2026-11-04): Observations are ordered by refinement (q' refines q when ker q' ⊆ ker q).
- [D10](decisions.md#d10-determinism) (2026-11-04): Whether a result is determined up to row order is reported by default and required by consumers that state it (incremental refreshes, idempotent sinks). Amended by [D48](decisions.md#d48-a-result-is-determined-up-to-the-classes-its-observation-reads), [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48).
- [D9](decisions.md#d9-one-tool-per-option) (2026-11-04): Each option gets one tool in the reference (raise, choose a value, or leave open), recorded in one table with its precondition and shim count.
- [D8](decisions.md#d8-the-references-null-order) (2026-11-04): Where a key's direction is UNSPECIFIED, the reference puts NULLs last in both directions.
- [D6](decisions.md#d6-sort-keys-without-a-null-order) (2026-11-04): A sort key's UNSPECIFIED direction leaves the NULL order open in the core, and CLUSTERED is an order relation of its own.
- [D5](decisions.md#d5-rounding-of-doubles-where-the-plan-omits-it) (2026-11-04): The core leaves the five rounding modes open; the reference and every engine profile fix TIE_TO_EVEN.
- [D4](decisions.md#d4-nan-payloads) (2026-11-04): A NaN is one of two codes, by its sign; no modelled function reads a payload.
- [D3](decisions.md#d3-behaviours-outside-the-yaml) (2026-11-04): An option's core set is the YAML's values plus the behaviours the engine facts record, each tagged with its fact.
- [D2](decisions.md#d2-raises-in-the-core) (2026-11-04): The core may raise everywhere, and each profile states its raise set.
- [U-2026-11-03-sum-overflow-row-bounds](#u-2026-11-03-sum-overflow-row-bounds) (2026-11-03): Row-count bounds get priority; integer SUM overflow becomes an inferred precondition checked on data, which row bounds can discharge statically.
- [U-2026-11-03-strings-2pow32](#u-2026-11-03-strings-2pow32) (2026-11-03): Reference strings have at most 2^32 characters, and substring with a start or length outside that range raises, for constants and columns alike. Amended by [U-2026-11-08-substring-limit-reference-profile](#u-2026-11-08-substring-limit-reference-profile).
- [U-2026-11-03-principle-3-reasonable-effort](#u-2026-11-03-principle-3-reasonable-effort) (2026-11-03): Reference principle 3 reads "Every engine can follow it with reasonable effort": each choice must be implementable on every engine with a reasonable shim or translation.
- [U-2026-11-03-no-avg-clamp](#u-2026-11-03-no-avg-clamp) (2026-11-03): AVG of doubles gets no clamp.
- [U-2026-11-03-fp32-modelled](#u-2026-11-03-fp32-modelled) (2026-11-03): fp32 (IEEE binary32) is to be modelled properly - arithmetic, casts and aggregates - instead of refused.
- [D37](decisions.md#d37-no-operation-creates-a-nan) (2026-11-02): No operation creates a NaN; a NaN from non-NaN inputs is an issue (Issue.domain).
- [U-2026-11-01-reference-principles](#u-2026-11-01-reference-principles) (2026-11-01): The reference is Substrait's semantics with each function's options, the least surprising choice where Substrait is open, fixed declared widths, emulable on every backend, errors as may-raise so rewrites are refinements. Amended by [U-2026-11-03-principle-3-reasonable-effort](#u-2026-11-03-principle-3-reasonable-effort).
- [U-2026-11-01-reference-open-choices](#u-2026-11-01-reference-open-choices) (2026-11-01): Reference choices where Substrait is open - modulus TRUNCATE, casts to integer truncate, substring negative start wraps from the end, one zero, lexicographic list order, avg of integers follows the plan's output type.
- [U-2026-11-01-omitted-option-is-error](#u-2026-11-01-omitted-option-is-error) (2026-11-01): When a plan omits overflow or on_division_by_zero, the reference raises (ERROR at the declared width); SILENT, SATURATE and NULL apply only when the plan asks.
- [D36](decisions.md#d36-doubles-have-one-zero-and-one-nan) (2026-10-31): The reference reads doubles with one zero and one NaN where rows are compared for identity.

### API

- [F192](#f192) (2026-11-20): Following the user's lenses choice, Python (Report/Route.lenses), report JSON (format 4), Substrait manifest (format 3) and OpenLineage facet (format 2) rename to lenses with readers for earlier formats; theory names stay.
- [F190](#f190) (2026-11-20): Memory limits: no sverify default, no Polars cap at 1.0, no RefreshReport.memory_limit; one public MemoryLimitError (code memory_limit), checker cap a subclass, CLI exit BUDGET; two settings stay.
- [F189](#f189) (2026-11-20): Contract drift ships as `sverify check --against-sink`: a committed promise is weakened when the fresh node does not prove it (text match as fast path, checker otherwise); codes contract_weakened (error) and plan_changed (note); JSON key drift.
- [F184](#f184) (2026-11-20): API-5 Report members: drop Report.warnings (and likely Report.errors); CheckedCondition.needs replaces suggested (#701 law under on_inferred=error); Report.facts as ColumnFacts data (#704); items 5-6 accepted. Amended by [F192](#f192).
- [F182](#f182) (2026-11-20): #1935 re-review at fb996b63: R1-R6 of ruling 180 applied; signed and ready for a merge batch.
- [F180](#f180) (2026-11-20): #1935 (public API closure) is signed after R1-R6: hints not dropped silently, walk covers MRO, sverify candidate wins, MACHINERY set in tests, CLIENT_VERSION unlisted, nodes.Sink protocol deleted; 12 TableCondition names get underscores later.
- [F175](#f175) (2026-11-20): Public = names in a public module's __all__ plus non-underscore members of its classes; held types go to a new re-export module sverify.types (18 names), top level stays at 19; five version constants leave __all__; PR B removes all deprecated spellings. Amended by [F180](#f180).
- [U-2026-11-20-report-lenses](#u-2026-11-20-report-lenses) (2026-11-20): The user chose `Report.lenses` as the one name for a report's lens settings (over observation and equivalence).
- [F150](#f150) (2026-11-19): Seven optimizer rules go on by default after #1829 (filterImplied, filterRuledOut, distinctKey, groupByKey, distinctAggKey, joinForeignKeyUnused, leftKeyUnused); rewrites run by default only where the run checked the preconditions (C1 to C5).
- [F140](#f140) (2026-11-19): F2 item 4: the report field listing black boxes and their declared laws is `Report.black_boxes` (name to laws mapping, one law encoder); F2 does not create `Report.assumptions`, which stays #1288's to build; verdict does not read it.
- [F139](#f139) (2026-11-19): #1285: `Rel.DeterminedUpTo` is centred on `r.eval`; no `Report.verified`, `Report.deterministic` means determined up to the observation; `strings=ascii` level defined by Unicode compatibility caseless key (3a now); 3b reserved D60; times default stays exact. Amended by [F144](#f144), [F149](#f149).
- [F126](#f126) (2026-11-19): `construct` is the keyword-form category on both `UnsupportedError` and `DialectDifference`; `sql` holds the as-written text (always set on `DialectDifference`); `key` stays the Profile field; one helper names a node's category.
- [F124](#f124) (2026-11-19): #1744 at 9a2b6fad accepted: decimal hint points at #322, one _rounding predicate refuses round with a digit count, tests and where path in, CI green.
- [F118](#f118) (2026-11-19): sv.connect("spark") exists; table conditions are counted as plans run by the engine's own runner (option b), not via Ibis's backend; no Ibis fallback; one plan per table; extras later drop sverify[duckdb].
- [F115](#f115) (2026-11-19): Named-exits API: ConditionError(UnsupportedError) with errors mapping; UnsupportedError.construct and .sql; Report.facts becomes dict of ColumnFacts and Report.not_null is removed; CheckedCondition.needs last. Order #699, #700, #704, #701. Amended by [F126](#f126).
- [F114](#f114) (2026-11-19): Report.output states the run's integer widths from outputKinds: int64 for computed integers, declared width for passed-through columns, the named width for explicit casts (writer writes the cast); casting the run to Ibis's widths is declined; timestamps normalised to timestamp(6).
- [F111](#f111) (2026-11-19): #1744 (D54 refusal of rounding casts) accepted in shape with three changes: decimal hint names no spelling (ruling 109 stands), one predicate refuses CAST(round(x, d)) on both paths, a test for the where values.
- [F109](#f109) (2026-11-19): The decimal cast refusal's hint points at #322 and names no spelling; the double cast hint names round, floor and ceil (no trunc); (b)'s PR adds no decimal rounding and no TRUNC; #1744 accepted with float_to_int/decimal_to_int leaving the differences table in (b)'s PR.
- [F105](#f105) (2026-11-19): DuckDB SQL under the default semantics: semantics='reference' stays the default (C rejected); principle L (the lift refines the dialect) reserved as D54; Report gains a dialect-differences list (verdict unchanged); no opt-out flag; corr_degenerate a listed exception. Amended by [F109](#f109), [U-2026-11-19-rounding-cast-refusal](#u-2026-11-19-rounding-cast-refusal).
- [F104](#f104) (2026-11-19): A decision on a source-marked value is a verdict-level error issue (`source_threshold`) in 12d-4, closing #1735; binding conditions C-rows, C-out, C-contract and taint rules M1-M3; D52 gains a dated sentence. Amended by [F108](#f108).
- [F103](#f103) (2026-11-19): sv.non_overlapping(lo, hi, *, by=()): NULL hi is the open end, by NULLs equal, zero-length allowed; held beside the keys in a bundle replacing K; a range-join single-match rule for inner/left joins, no closed-endpoint rule; native data check; dbt mutually_exclusive_ranges mapping. Amended by [F134](#f134).
- [F98](#f98) (2026-11-19): Spark FETCH and windows over a named table: neither refusal option A nor --ties mode B; to_sql's rows default follows the dialect plugin's ProfilePlugin.rows (multiset for Spark); rows='sequence' on Spark stays refused where it needs an ordinal. Amended by [F107](#f107).
- [F96](#f96) (2026-11-19): A sink records engine, engine version, sverify version and profile outside the digest; across an engine change deterministic delta views resume with a note and open-valued delta views recompute once; a changed profile recomputes via its own plan_digest component.
- [F90](#f90) (2026-11-19): Report.output takes decimal types from the checker: the contract JSON gains outputKinds from Write.relKinds (no new theorem), so a decimal SUM of decimal(10,2) reports DECIMAL(20,2); conditions K1 to K8. Amended by [F114](#f114).
- [U-2026-11-19-default-observation](#u-2026-11-19-default-observation) (2026-11-19): The default observation is multiset and canon: PLAN_BOUND = Observation(floats='plan', zeros='canon', elements='multiset'); it sets the level Report.deterministic is read at and nothing more.
- [F-1642-d48-python](#f-1642-d48-python) (2026-11-18): #1642 (D48 parts 3-4): plan-level refusal at a class pick accepted (with text fixes noting DuckDB's stable-pick row); _resolve unchanged. New D48 step 5 (engine stable pick) and step 6 (refinement order reads the class predicate).
- [F-1641-d46-run-check](#f-1641-d46-run-check) (2026-11-18): #1641's D46 range check is right; five changes before review (runs also with check_requires=False, resident tables, every baseSchema, unsigned tests, D46 states what holds at merge). D46 (B) gated on step A2; date64 leaves widens. Amended by [F65](#f65).
- [F-1632-margin-open-choices](#f-1632-margin-open-choices) (2026-11-18): A margin check above a FETCH or tied window, where check and run are separate statements without the row ordinal (rows='multiset' engines), refuses by default and warns under on_inferred='warn'; no engine-name branch in report.py.
- [F72](#f72) (2026-11-18): The plan digest hashes a record (a tuple, not a counter, not named 'epoch') of changes that make an unchanged plan compute another result; each decision above D49 states its kind in docs/decisions.md, checked by a test. No D-number.
- [F67](#f67) (2026-11-18): #1608 item 12 (a) and (b) go ahead with corrections (exact form qty*COUNT(qty) > SUM(qty), no lift rewrite); correlated [NOT] EXISTS lifts as semi/anti join under nine conditions; correlated IN/NOT IN may follow. Amended by [F71](#f71), [F82](#f82).
- [F65](#f65) (2026-11-18): #1658 (D46 step A2): a transform on bound tables needs no run check; a view's first load calls refuse_schema. #1658 meets the A2 gate for #1643 once green. Follow-up A3 adds non-null checks on Scan/Resident.
- [U-2026-11-16-report-verdict](#u-2026-11-16-report-verdict) (2026-11-16): Report.verdict replaces Report.proved with no alias, taking the values proved, proved_with_checks or unproved, and bool(verdict) raises.
- [U-2026-11-09-ties-pinned-only](#u-2026-11-09-ties-pinned-only) (2026-11-09): Only the deterministic profile or reproducible= pins tie order; otherwise the engine picks and a rerun may pick another.
- [U-2026-11-09-sql-file-cli](#u-2026-11-09-sql-file-cli) (2026-11-09): The CLI verifies a bare .sql file, with the SQL dialect front-end a field on ProfilePlugin and DuckDB the only in-tree dialect for now.
- [U-2026-11-09-portable-semantics-name](#u-2026-11-09-portable-semantics-name) (2026-11-09): The user-facing API says semantics="portable" with no reference synonym, while Profile.reference stays the Lean theory term.
- [U-2026-11-09-one-verb-verify](#u-2026-11-09-one-verb-verify) (2026-11-09): verify is the one verb for plans built in Python or loaded from Substrait, recheck is dropped, and from_substrait is a constructor.
- [U-2026-11-09-lenses-in-contract](#u-2026-11-09-lenses-in-contract) (2026-11-09): The user-facing term is lens, lenses live in the contract's postconditions rather than as verify parameters, and equivalent and deterministic are verdicts read off the result.
- [U-2026-11-09-lens-defaults](#u-2026-11-09-lens-defaults) (2026-11-09): Default lenses are floats within epsilon, rows as a multiset with order opt-in, zeros and NaN canon, strings exact and errors exact. Amended by [U-2026-11-19-default-observation](#u-2026-11-19-default-observation).
- [U-2026-11-09-determinism-result-attribute](#u-2026-11-09-determinism-result-attribute) (2026-11-09): Determinism and row order are attributes of the result computed per observable, not parameters of verify or of the profile.
- [U-2026-11-09-1288-d5-no-deprecations](#u-2026-11-09-1288-d5-no-deprecations) (2026-11-09): #1288 decision 5: every deprecation alias goes now, none carried past 1.0.
- [U-2026-11-08-verify-under-any-profile](#u-2026-11-08-verify-under-any-profile) (2026-11-08): verify runs under any profile, reference to engine with shims or engine to engine natively with semantics=X, and _PROFILE_REQUESTS stays.
- [U-2026-11-06-unordered-fetch-refresh](#u-2026-11-06-unordered-fetch-refresh) (2026-11-06): Unordered FETCH is allowed in verify and run but refused in refresh unless deterministic or reproducible.
- [U-2026-11-06-smt-certificates-proved](#u-2026-11-06-smt-certificates-proved) (2026-11-06): SMT certificates count as proved with their axioms listed, and conservation is proved or checked.
- [U-2026-11-05-verify-suggestions](#u-2026-11-05-verify-suggestions) (2026-11-05): Reports suggest declarations that make a plan verify (max_rows, IS NOT NULL, unique), under on_inferred where they are preconditions.
- [U-2026-11-05-unproved-refused-route-auto](#u-2026-11-05-unproved-refused-route-auto) (2026-11-05): run and refresh refuse unproved plans, route="auto" is the default, and an explicit override runs them.
- [U-2026-11-05-sink-float-default](#u-2026-11-05-sink-float-default) (2026-11-05): Sinks accept doubles within the plan's error bound by default, and exact bits are opt-in.
- [U-2026-11-05-scd-as-transforms](#u-2026-11-05-scd-as-transforms) (2026-11-05): Slowly changing dimensions are supported as proved transforms over a change-feed source, not as a feature of their own. Amended by [U-2026-11-19-scd2-into-1-0](#u-2026-11-19-scd2-into-1-0).
- [U-2026-11-05-report-json-sarif-shape](#u-2026-11-05-report-json-sarif-shape) (2026-11-05): The report JSON stays its own format, but its findings take SARIF's result shape so --format sarif is a thin wrapper.
- [U-2026-11-05-observation-naming](#u-2026-11-05-observation-naming) (2026-11-05): User-facing names drop q for observation and observation model, approx is an observation, and route classes are exact, observed, shim and refused. Amended by [U-2026-11-09-lenses-in-contract](#u-2026-11-09-lenses-in-contract), [U-2026-11-20-report-lenses](#u-2026-11-20-report-lenses).
- [U-2026-11-05-for-enterprise-naming](#u-2026-11-05-for-enterprise-naming) (2026-11-05): The deploy path on an organisation's own infrastructure is called for enterprise, and the GitHub-only path is everything in your repository.
- [U-2026-11-05-execution-bounded-by-memory](#u-2026-11-05-execution-bounded-by-memory) (2026-11-05): The CLI's 30 s timeout never bounds query execution, which is bounded by memory limits.
- [U-2026-11-05-drop-junit](#u-2026-11-05-drop-junit) (2026-11-05): sverify check --format junit is dropped before 1.x freezes the command.
- [U-2026-11-05-cli-timeout-30s](#u-2026-11-05-cli-timeout-30s) (2026-11-05): The CLI's default --timeout is 30 s for every command, bounding proof, planning and checking only, never query execution; execution is bounded by memory.
- [U-2026-11-04-substrait-export-contract-metadata](#u-2026-11-04-substrait-export-contract-metadata) (2026-11-04): to_substrait() carries requires and ensures as metadata in the exported plan, declared and inferred preconditions alike, in user terms and machine-readable form.
- [D23](decisions.md#d23-inferred-preconditions-are-errors-by-default) (2026-11-04): An inferred precondition that rules out a runtime error is a checking error by default, named with its fixes.
- [D22](decisions.md#d22-four-route-classes-and-tolerances-are-observations) (2026-11-04): A route's class is one of exact, observed, shim or refused, and tolerances are observations.
- [D20](decisions.md#d20-two-observations-per-output) (2026-11-04): Each output has two observations, q_post for correctness and q_det for determinism, checked independently. Amended by [F-c2-determinism-d48](#f-c2-determinism-d48).
- [D13](decisions.md#d13-choosing-the-route) (2026-11-04): verify and run choose the route by default (native, translated, transfer check), name it in the result, and semantics= or an explicit route overrides.
- [D12](decisions.md#d12-contracts-and-the-observation) (2026-11-04): The user states the observation q; other ensures are checked on q's canonical representative or rejected.
- [D11](decisions.md#d11-postconditions-name-the-equivalence) (2026-11-04): Each output's postcondition names the equivalence its consumer expects (multiset or sequence; floats within ε, exact or a stated tolerance).
- [U-2026-11-03-reference-default-semantics](#u-2026-11-03-reference-default-semantics) (2026-11-03): The reference semantics is the default everywhere, and DuckDB's semantics is an explicit opt-in via semantics=. Amended by [U-2026-11-09-one-verb-verify](#u-2026-11-09-one-verb-verify).
- [U-2026-11-02-sql-text-reads-as-duckdb](#u-2026-11-02-sql-text-reads-as-duckdb) (2026-11-02): DuckDB SQL text reads as DuckDB's in both semantics modes (division, integer division, NULLs last, literal and decimal typing); semantics only changes what functions do where DuckDB and the reference differ.
- [U-2026-11-02-params-bind-at-verify](#u-2026-11-02-params-bind-at-verify) (2026-11-02): verify(params=...) binds ibis.param values at verification time (option A).
- [U-2026-11-02-memory-guarantee-ux](#u-2026-11-02-memory-guarantee-ux) (2026-11-02): Bounding row counts and memory is a goal; the user-facing promise is "this pipeline won't run out of memory", with sharding and further budget tightening as means.

### Engines

- [F199](#f199) (2026-11-20): #2051: Lean carries no version; profiles are indexed by a behaviour record built only on first unresolvable value difference; facts record versions as points with per-version probe-run records; rules R1-R3 bound fragile native rows; services range over their major version. Amended by [U-2026-11-20-minor-range](#u-2026-11-20-minor-range).
- [F198.addendum](#f198addendum) (2026-11-20): After the list_contains value-side drift at DuckDB 2.0.1: D70's fourth paragraph amended so a version-agnostic lowering comes first; K2 amended to rely on the full engine suite; list_contains gets a DuckDB shim, reference unchanged.
- [F198](#f198) (2026-11-20): D70 signed, amending D14: a probed engine version claims in full, an unprobed version in the probed minor warns and is marked on the route, outside the range is refused; package bounds equal the range; no guarantee rests on an engine raise; daily canary. Amended by [F198.addendum](#f198addendum), [F199](#f199), [U-2026-11-20-engine-versions](#u-2026-11-20-engine-versions).
- [F185](#f185) (2026-11-20): DuckDB's tracking rows may cite the generated lowering theorems under a per-group evidence rule (C1-C6); decimal rows stay tested and tracking, with a guard that no dec. row is proved.
- [F174](#f174) (2026-11-20): E7 of note 167 becomes: DuckDB, Polars and Spark run the exact limbs (Spark probed); Snowflake keeps E7 as written; fold-back option A declined. One binary split on every engine; E2's per-row check reworded to a normal-form check. No D-number.
- [D70](#d70) (2026-11-20): A probed engine version claims in full, an unprobed version within the probed minor warns, and a version outside the range is refused; package bounds equal the range. Amended by [F198.addendum](#f198addendum), [F199](#f199), [U-2026-11-20-engine-versions](#u-2026-11-20-engine-versions).
- [F170](#f170) (2026-11-19): The Polars join_where swap of rulings 162/164 is dropped on its measurements (cross join plus filter is faster and smaller); a facts-and-docs PR follows (fact polars-join-where, D19 note); #1857 is a Polars wrong-result bug needing an owner.
- [F167](#f167) (2026-11-19): Note 167: conditions E1-E8 for the exact integer-limb state (overflow guard, checked mantissa split, limb lemma under `no_drift`, trust-base update, bounded finish, property test, engine rollout, bit-exact native finish); `no_drift` keeps its statement. Amended by [F174](#f174).
- [F164.A1](#f164a1) (2026-11-19): The Polars join_where swap is dropped after homelab measurements showed the old path faster and smaller; leftovers are a polars-join-where fact and the D19 note without join_where. #1857 (Polars left join returns where a conjunct raises) is a bug.
- [F163](#f163) (2026-11-19): Split fp.elem: power goes to fp.pow (with pow_one_inf) and atan2 to fp.atan2, so unary functions no longer inherit Spark's pow_one_inf refusal; plain removal of pow_one_inf refused. No reference value or allowed set changes; no D-number.
- [F162](#f162) (2026-11-19): ASOF join (#1447 item 6): no new core node (C rejected); the pair cap stays as interim; native DuckDB ASOF JOIN admitted under A1-A8 (one fact, Lean shape recognition, cap moved to the run); item stays open until A and the run-time count land. Amended by [F164](#f164), [F170](#f170).
- [F161](#f161) (2026-11-19): Spark's IEEE division CASE that gave the wrong infinity at a -0.0 divisor is a bug: Spark writes plain '/', and a zero divisor raises as the model states; the same holds for Snowflake.
- [F160](#f160) (2026-11-19): Remedy for ruling 158's open Snowflake case, with no writer change and a public admission gate test; details in the private evidence. Amended by [F161](#f161).
- [F158](#f158) (2026-11-19): Snowflake's fold-against-execute rows stand as classified, and a claim at an up-to row holds up to its class; one open case is answered by ruling 160; details in the private evidence.
- [F157](#f157) (2026-11-19): #1818's log measurements are accepted with ruling 132's constants; Polars's fp.div precondition is text only and two wrong proved bounds on main (subnormal literal divisor, non-literal constant divisor) are a separate bug, F1 to F5.
- [F147](#f147) (2026-11-19): Snowflake tie picks need no change, and 'reachable' is redefined as a property of input values; details in the private evidence. Amended by [F158](#f158).
- [F145](#f145) (2026-11-19): Snowflake's zero sign after rounding functions needs no shim and no refusal; the private profile states it within the reference's open option; details in the private evidence. Amended by [F147](#f147).
- [F132](#f132) (2026-11-19): Spark's ln, log10, log2, log(b,x) and trig of doubles run natively under per-call ulp bounds (Spark's spec number plus reference's), sin/cos/tan guarded at |x| <= 2^20; Spark default no bound; per-engine numbers move to `EngineProfile` data.
- [F131](#f131) (2026-11-19): DuckDB implicit coercions: lift implements DuckDB's integer-literal width rule on a probed allowlist; NULLIF returns `a` itself; decimal +/- gets a checked operand rescale (D56 drafted, held for user); TIMESTAMPTZ mixes refused in the lift; facts PR first.
- [F123](#f123) (2026-11-19): The #1773 deviation from ruling 121 is accepted; after merge, check that the Arrow cast is safe=True.
- [F122](#f122) (2026-11-19): Spark cross-scale decimal comparison past 38 digits is refused (#1777); an exact FLOOR/CEIL same-scale shim follows before 1.0 after a Spark fact probe; upstream candidate recorded.
- [F121](#f121) (2026-11-19): The Polars runner casts passed-through narrow integer columns back to their declared width in #1773 so report.output matches the run; explicit casts' widths stay with #1772. Amended by [F123](#f123).
- [F110](#f110) (2026-11-19): Declined: the upscaled side of a cross-scale decimal comparison keeps dkind 38 s, not the checked decimal; DuckDB's writer compares exactly since #1523; allowed an rfl lemma that evaluation ignores the precision; Spark needs its own fact and writer follow-up.
- [F107](#f107) (2026-11-19): #1743 (Spark total sort/fetch) follows ruling 98's design but is not ready: CI red; snapshot, coverage and catalogue records move with the multiset default; Snowflake default also multiset; build reports must state CI state.
- [F100](#f100) (2026-11-19): Constant-only guarded operands on Polars (P1 of #1719): no guard and no column tie; D21 already refuses every plan with a raising constant subexpression; P1 restated (K1), binder copies operands whole (K2), Polars fact extended (K3), checks K4 and K5. Amended by [F112](#f112).
- [F87](#f87) (2026-11-19): #1680 merges under (a) of ruling 75 with a line on #1329's 1.0 list (Spark refresh cost measured and stated, closes in N6c); the 2x gate was mis-set. N14 decided: the Polars store takes the parts form via one shared module. Amended by [F88](#f88).
- [F-1639-driver-footers](#f-1639-driver-footers) (2026-11-18): #1639 (N5) accepted as an interim: the Spark driver reads Parquet footers only for local paths on a shared filesystem; other cases follow ruling 3 or raise Unsupported. New step N12 (route A opens no data file in the driver) required before 1.0.
- [F-1620-arrow-fallback](#f-1620-arrow-fallback) (2026-11-18): #1620 accepted for a local engine: where DuckDB cannot load httpfs/aws, a Delta table reads through Arrow as on main; sverify runs no INSTALL. Adds step N11 (report names each table's read route) before 1.0.
- [F-1608-sql-ordinals](#f-1608-sql-ordinals) (2026-11-18): The SQL writer drops the rowid ordinal outside rows='sequence' (proposal A under conditions A1-A4: one arrangement per window node, ORDER BY reaching LIMIT, one SQL choice per open choice); B accepted with order_matters derived from the root's chain. No D-number. Amended by [F-1632-margin-open-choices](#f-1632-margin-open-choices).
- [F82](#f82) (2026-11-18): 12d-2 builds with corrections A1-A5; no `Profile.duckdb` row for avg sets; 12b splits into 12b-1 (reference path exact) and 12b-2 (native DuckDB mean); new step 12d-4 surfaces the set at the lift. Amended by [F85](#f85), [F92](#f92).
- [F79](#f79) (2026-11-18): Profile.duckdb's integer AVG becomes a set: either neighbour of the exact quotient for |s| <= 2^53, a relative bound c*2^-53 above; #1675 may merge as a facts PR; step 12d-2 adds DuckAgg.Allowed and three lemmas, no regeneration. No D-number. Amended by [F82](#f82).
- [F77](#f77) (2026-11-18): DuckDB, Spark and Polars run D50's lifted plan on recorded CASE-laziness facts (Polars with CSE off); a shape or engine without such a fact refuses; Snowflake per its private facts. Two Polars shapes (aggregate FILTER, window) get tests.
- [F75](#f75) (2026-11-18): Spark store refresh (#1680): a SparkState is a list of parts; merge with keep writes the change as a part and consolidates later via the Lean merge plan; (c) two stores declined; acceptance at most 2x main's refresh time, else (a) with a 1.0-list line. Amended by [F87](#f87).
- [F74](#f74) (2026-11-18): DuckDB's integer AVG depends on plan shape and platform; the DuckDB profile's avg(BIGINT) allows a set of doubles stated over every shape (no fact names a shape). #1675's label held until its texts say so; steps 12d-1/2/3 set. No D-number. Amended by [F79](#f79).
- [F71](#f71) (2026-11-18): 12d answered: DuckDB's profile integer mean (one double division) stands and no longer gates semantics='duckdb' on Linux; targeted probe pairs and a facts PR for duckdb-avg-int.json close the fact. Amended by [F74](#f74).
- [F68](#f68) (2026-11-18): D51 facts on #1649: L1 and L4 accepted, L3 accepted on the type, L2 accepted as a statement without evidence; the D51 Lean/Python PR records L2 per pyspark and conf, makes L3's checks able to fail, and names ICU versions.
- [F66](#f66) (2026-11-18): Spark's case mapping: no locale precondition; Spark's writer pins the case locale in the plan by lowering non-ASCII UPPER/LOWER to collate(upper(collate(x,'UNICODE')),'UTF8_BINARY'); the guard narrows once facts L1-L4 are recorded. No D-number. Amended by [F68](#f68).
- [F64](#f64) (2026-11-18): The Polars translator gains rows='multiset' (plain group-by, no maintain_order, canonical float keys); PolarsStore.merge passes it. Options (2) DuckDB merge and (3) hand-written consolidation declined; (1) is a fallback. Follow-up N13. No D-number.
- [D49](decisions.md#d49-a-value-known-from-one-engine-is-provisional) (2026-11-18): A value known from DuckDB's facts alone is DuckDB-defined: it runs on DuckDB only until other engines are probed.
- [U-2026-11-17-native-table-reads](#u-2026-11-17-native-table-reads) (2026-11-17): Table reads happen natively in each engine, never through the Python driver, and native scans #173 move into 1.0 M2.
- [U-2026-11-15-polars-pending-until-271](#u-2026-11-15-polars-pending-until-271) (2026-11-15): A semantics="polars" request is reported as pending until the generated Polars lowering (#271) lands.
- [U-2026-11-09-drop-datafusion](#u-2026-11-09-drop-datafusion) (2026-11-09): DataFusion is removed from sverify entirely, with its upstream bug entries kept as history.
- [U-2026-11-08-snowflake-low-priority](#u-2026-11-08-snowflake-low-priority) (2026-11-08): Snowflake is low priority, with no push to record or fuzz it.
- [U-2026-11-08-no-quadratic-algorithms](#u-2026-11-08-no-quadratic-algorithms) (2026-11-08): No quadratic algorithms run anywhere a data library processes user data.
- [U-2026-11-08-no-python-in-engine-paths](#u-2026-11-08-no-python-in-engine-paths) (2026-11-08): Engine execution paths use native operations only, with no Python functions, and an approximate observable covers what cannot be exact.
- [U-2026-11-08-no-bit-identical-limb-sums](#u-2026-11-08-no-bit-identical-limb-sums) (2026-11-08): Bit-identical batch double sums via integer limbs are declined on cost, and compensated window sums go after 1.0. Amended by [U-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state](#u-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state).
- [U-2026-11-08-facts-first-guarantees](#u-2026-11-08-facts-first-guarantees) (2026-11-08): Engine guarantees follow the engine's known behaviour, read from its source and recorded as facts, rather than assuming it cannot match the reference.
- [U-2026-11-07-snowflake-profile-private](#u-2026-11-07-snowflake-profile-private) (2026-11-07): The Snowflake profile is private, and public sverify gets only engine-agnostic hooks for out-of-tree profiles.
- [U-2026-11-05-snowflake-evidence-private](#u-2026-11-05-snowflake-evidence-private) (2026-11-05): Snowflake evidence stays in a private repository, and public sverify gets only its own Snowflake SQL writer fixes.
- [U-2026-11-05-polars-evaluable-profile](#u-2026-11-05-polars-evaluable-profile) (2026-11-05): Polars gets a full evaluable profile with native runs under semantics="polars".
- [U-2026-11-04-polars-streaming](#u-2026-11-04-polars-streaming) (2026-11-04): sverify targets Polars's streaming engine, with facts probed under streaming and a forced in-memory engine refused.
- [U-2026-11-04-polars-pl3-division-pow](#u-2026-11-04-polars-pl3-division-pow) (2026-11-04): Polars keeps its own division (approx within 2 ulps, with a subnormal-divisor guard); a negative base, -inf included, to a fractional power is guarded as a reference domain error, not shimmed.
- [U-2026-11-04-characterization-checklist](#u-2026-11-04-characterization-checklist) (2026-11-04): The characterization checklist (areas, facts' options, coverage) defines what an engine characterization records.
- [D16](decisions.md#d16-engine-correctness) (2026-11-04): An engine runs a plan correctly when it returns a result the reference allows or raises only where the reference may; resource errors are excluded.
- [D14](decisions.md#d14-engine-versions) (2026-11-04): An engine profile records the versions its facts were probed at; another version warns and another major version is refused. Amended by [D70](#d70), [F198](#f198), [U-2026-11-20-engine-versions](#u-2026-11-20-engine-versions).
- [U-2026-11-02-duckdb-mode-models-nan](#u-2026-11-02-duckdb-mode-models-nan) (2026-11-02): Under semantics="duckdb" NaN and infinity are modelled as DuckDB computes them, never refused, and verify decides per contract.
- [U-2026-11-02-duckdb-lead-target](#u-2026-11-02-duckdb-lead-target) (2026-11-02): The verified SQL pipeline is planned with DuckDB as the lead target.

### Architecture

- [F197](#f197) (2026-11-20): An integer or decimal SUM that fits its type on a proved row bound is proved exact in colEps, via the single check Measure.sumFits (intSumExact removed); window sums and iavg stay unproved.
- [F196](#f196) (2026-11-20): One functional-dependency analysis (P) approved: one FDOn, one Rel.fds, full fdClosure in analysis/FunctionalDeps.lean; equality dependencies stay in the keys walk; conditions P1-P9.
- [F195](#f195) (2026-11-20): #2018 is fixed in 1.0 by a check plan whose verdicts are values an engine statement returns (option B); optimizer-settings fix (A) and the DuckDB pre-run (C) rejected; reference unchanged.
- [F194](#f194) (2026-11-20): Functional dependencies for #1992/#1993: agreement up to canonV (FDOn, membership form), one walk with Rel.keys, one transfer lemma; exactly-one for how=left is a separate later theorem; no D-number. Amended by [F196](#f196).
- [F183](#f183) (2026-11-20): D50-d work order accepted: d3b+d3d now on the three-copy chain, then d1 and d3c, then d3e, under conditions O1-O4; d1 stays in the plan.
- [F179](#f179) (2026-11-20): #1876's W6 round trip is fixed in the same PR: .dtrunc (and .dround if cheap) map the interval monotonically in the transfer; .decimal's transfer stays id; no D-number.
- [F178](#f178) (2026-11-20): #1873 (exact limb state) is signed with condition R1 (attach checks zero-count and group-membership invariants) and changes R2-R4 (drop dead b=10 split step, keep rebuild figure, fix wording).
- [F176](#f176) (2026-11-20): A1 for Arrow-fed sources takes (b): one store-copy path for every source, Source docstring corrected, measurement with a minimal table-free source; io.Source's held table counts as held by sverify. (a) recorded for later. Clamp case closed. Amended by [U-2026-11-20-io-source-gate](#u-2026-11-20-io-source-gate).
- [F173](#f173) (2026-11-20): The pruned copy of an Arrow-fed input is a State of the engine's Store (the maintained view project S t), fixed at a seal after the view is made; refuse at registration where a snapshot is missing, never keep-all. StaticSource keeps its API. No D-number. Amended by [F176](#f176).
- [F-epic-proven-optimizations](#f-epic-proven-optimizations) (2026-11-19): Proved optimizations epic: proved rewrites (setup 1) are the default and cover 1.0; checked rewrites (setup 2) only for global search, reusing #958's normal form; refinement is the statement; writers gain no decisions; per-rule measured-gain gate; ordered work list.
- [F169](#f169) (2026-11-19): Withdraws K5's side lemma; Rel.floatOpenSafeP_of_noDouble takes the decidable hypothesis r.closedB = true, the gate checks it, and integer BIT_* and QUANTILE_DISC plans are refused; widening closedB to .bit is a later PR.
- [F166](#f166) (2026-11-19): Bind moves to the frontend as Sverify.Frontend.Bind; Outcome.bind_error, Outcome.ok_bind and Expr.evalP_subst move unchanged to core Profile.lean; Expr.readsFieldFrom/readsField stay in Parse.lean. No D-number.
- [F159](#f159) (2026-11-19): #900's Python step is approved in direction: refinement and plan bound move into Realize.lean, the checker emits the final class with coded problems, Python keeps wording; acceptance is a site count of zero, one PR in two commits, C1 to C6.
- [F154](#f154) (2026-11-19): Track B's F3 is approved: plans with no double transfer to the reference via Rel.transfer_of_noDouble under transferB on t.refAt floatOnlyOptions; one mayDouble/hasDouble family in core; no extras hypothesis; raisesOn in #1834 accepted. Amended by [F169](#f169), [F171](#f171).
- [F153](#f153) (2026-11-19): Accepts P1b's class-level predicate Relation.DoublesKinded and the bridge lemma; core has the typing fact (Rel.kinds_sound), so the discharge is follow-up P1c; ObsCtx.ofRelation is dropped in favour of an example. Amended by [F154](#f154).
- [F152](#f152) (2026-11-19): Rewrites apply wherever their verification holds: under semantics='duckdb' no rule theorem changes and the gate opens in RunPlan.lean (R1); pending profiles use the reference's gate; engine profiles need a refinement under one table after #271.
- [F151](#f151) (2026-11-19): Approves #1838's Lean move once M1 to M5 are met (repair docs/proofs.md, two test assertions, IssuesAll in the no-engine test, Refresh to analysis, two links); import-free modules are placed by their subject.
- [F149](#f149) (2026-11-19): #1828 keeps its scope; P1b follows as its own PR, strengthening check_deterministic_sound and check_class_determined_sound from OrderAllowed to Allowed with DeterminedUpTo corollaries (Obs.members, Obs.classes); no FloatOpenSafe side condition.
- [F148](#f148) (2026-11-19): Approves the #1444 root-move plan: Completeness goes to core, ProvedOn/Statements/IssuesAll stay in the root, OpenSums is split later (O2), and the acceptance becomes the ROOT_CHECKER/ROOT_THEOREMS/ROOT_WAITING sets in test_lean_layers.py. Amended by [F151](#f151).
- [F146](#f146) (2026-11-19): AVG leaves #1805, which states the corollary for a double SUM only; SumNear for AVG stays a named hypothesis; an unconditional AVG lemma (B1, B2) goes with track A's ruling 135 (b) follow-up.
- [F143](#f143) (2026-11-19): Approves the #1443 registry plan with five changes: keep the name EngineProfile for now (rename to ProfileRows later), side is a field recorded as sideName and printed, EngineEntry.rows, out-of-tree engines state an entry at their next pin bump.
- [F136](#f136) (2026-11-19): The optimizer's near gate may narrow to plans where a double can occur, if the check covers every expression, runs on s and r, names the open `FloatOpenSafeP` obligation and per-engine theorems; else it stays as in 129. Amended by [F150](#f150), [F154](#f154).
- [F134](#f134) (2026-11-19): #1795 unifies `Rel.keyedJoin` for Keys, Rows and AnalysisCongr only; `joinElim` and `eagerAgg` stay key-only, range joins there go to the #1787 backlog after step 2.
- [F130](#f130) (2026-11-19): Track B #1484: F1 builds issues on every allowed order with `DataAssumed` taking `SmallAll`; F2 keeps the black-box clamp (no axiom, no D56), the checker reports law breaks and laws; F3 builds root-aggregate `FloatOpenSafeP`. Amended by [F135](#f135), [F140](#f140).
- [F129](#f129) (2026-11-19): #1787 step 2 built with eight conditions: `Rule` enum with per-rule default, exact ORDER BY shape for sequence, issue gate with per-rule `keepsIssues`, reads/functions subsets, reference profile only, foreign keys in the statement, `Small` per rule, one plan accessor. Amended by [F136](#f136).
- [F127](#f127) (2026-11-19): #1787 step 1 accepted: congruence lemmas for refinement in `Refines.lean`, `Engine.Sound.of_refines` and `contract_sound_rewrite`, `Engine.runPlan`; conditions: move `Measure.Allowed.perm` to core, document per-constructor lemma forms, add `contract_sound_runs_rewrite`, weaken `check` hypothesis. Lean only.
- [F120](#f120) (2026-11-19): Staging `Rel.evalChoices_allowed` behind a decidable `Rel.ChoicesSupported` is accepted with four conditions: PR 1 carries ruling 113 part 0, the predicate lives only in the theorem, each later PR removes one case, PR 2 handles aggregates with open cells.
- [F117](#f117) (2026-11-19): Measure.openB is obsolete after D25 and its open-sum machinery is unreachable; delete it (openB, aggCtx, near_eval, floatOpenSafe_aggregate and the postAllowed branch) in a small Lean PR separate from #1749.
- [F116](#f116) (2026-11-19): #1329's join line measures the join sides kept to match, not the materialized output; amendments: Arrow-fed inputs leave the driver, R2/R3 are one projection lemma over Z-sets, lake sources need a pinned-snapshot precondition. Amended by [F173](#f173).
- [F113](#f113) (2026-11-19): Rel.evalChoices: first fix exact-equality grouping/dedup to distinctRows; prove acceptance implies allowed (Rel.evalChoices_allowed, val matches by =, multiset); the proposed allowed-implies-accepted direction is false and recorded as the oracle's known gap. Amended by [F120](#f120).
- [F108](#f108) (2026-11-19): 12d-4: keys reading tainted columns are a `source_threshold` site (rule C-key); the S2(c) control is restated and `ratioSharpen` for marked quotients is a follow-up with a decidable side condition. D52 gains 'and keys'.
- [F102](#f102) (2026-11-19): D50-d design second part: one running three-valued column per guarded chain, levels pruned via emit, binder as a pure Lean frontend function with theorem T1; join/semi conditions and lambda bodies keep d1's chain and cap at 1.0; engines re-probed (R1-R4). Amended by [U-2026-11-19-d50-cap](#u-2026-11-19-d50-cap).
- [F95](#f95) (2026-11-19): D50-d3: the linear shape is the guarded column, each chain operand bound once in a projection with a reach column; no core form; d1's encoding stands as ruled in 94; design filed as issue #1719. Amended by [F102](#f102).
- [F94](#f94) (2026-11-19): D50-d inputs: step d1 rewrites the guard chain as CASE WHEN NOT p THEN FALSE ELSE p AND (...) END (two copies only where a raising operand follows); no checker sharing for 1.0; linear size undecided pending d1's table and a profile (d2). Amended by [F102](#f102), [F95](#f95).
- [U-2026-11-19-rewrites-every-profile](#u-2026-11-19-rewrites-every-profile) (2026-11-19): Proved rewrites apply under every profile whose analysis proves their preconditions, not only under the reference.
- [U-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state](#u-2026-11-19-exact-limb-state) (2026-11-19): Maintained double SUM, AVG, VAR, STDDEV, COVAR and CORR stay exact (D25, D30); the state moves into the engines as integer limbs; no compensated and no quantised state.
- [U-2026-11-19-auditability-standing](#u-2026-11-19-auditability-standing) (2026-11-19): Auditability is a standing concern: a bridge lemma, one read-only verdict function in Python, and a trusted-base page with a test.
- [F-a7-statements](#f-a7-statements) (2026-11-18): A7 Statements.lean: closed moves into engine conditions, a Checked layer under Verified, postcondition as a predicate on results; worked examples are #guards plus pointers to kernel examples; no check_optimized_sound corollary.
- [F-a6-statements](#f-a6-statements) (2026-11-18): A6 (PR #1623): check_proved_on and its three engine corollaries accepted; closed stays the float-safety field and underReference stays on the DuckDB corollary; five text conditions. Amended by [F-a7-statements](#f-a7-statements).
- [F-a5-statements](#f-a5-statements) (2026-11-18): A5 (#1484 finding 4): Rel.FloatOpenSafeP is the open-float hypothesis (not Engine.RefWithin); contract_sound_near is the core step; Engine.Runs loses tested; docs must say nothing discharges the hypothesis yet.
- [F-173-n7-sinks-order](#f-173-n7-sinks-order) (2026-11-18): #173: N6 (stores) comes before N7 (sinks), and N7 builds on the engine's real handles; Arrow-backed writers (A) and a DuckDB-only sink protocol (C) declined. Parquet-writer facts may start now.
- [F-1631-regexp-match-read](#f-1631-regexp-match-read) (2026-11-18): Blocker for #1631: regexp_match's row gets its constructor and the #guard checks the transfer's actual read of non_ascii_text instead of naming functions by hand; test a bare regexp_match projection is unproved under semantics='substrait'.
- [F81](#f81) (2026-11-18): Step 12e confirmed: COUNT's abstract upper bound comes from the input's row cap (`Measure.abs` count case), `abs_sound` keeps its statement; the cap reaches a subquery's COUNT except through relations without `maxRows`.
- [F80](#f80) (2026-11-18): D51 Lean/Python pre-build statements accepted: Text.upper/lower as definitions over the generated table; issue caseDiffers and option case_mapping; facts renamed upperAgreed/lowerAgreed with one per-column kinds list; ILIKE stays under D43's ASCII raise until fold facts exist. Amended by [F83](#f83).
- [F69](#f69) (2026-11-18): A refusal's exit must be a query the user can get proved; step 12e gives COUNT an upper bound from the row cap in Measure.abs. No D-number. Amended by [F70](#f70).
- [U-2026-11-09-engine-native-state-stores](#u-2026-11-09-engine-native-state-stores) (2026-11-09): The engine a pipeline runs on holds its state natively through its own store, ArrowStore is removed, and the state store is a required plugin part for incremental engines.
- [U-2026-11-09-engine-free-verify-only-base](#u-2026-11-09-engine-free-verify-only-base) (2026-11-09): Every engine, DuckDB included, is an optional extra; base sverify is verify-only, with store, scans, pipeline state and IO behind the engine extras. Amended by [U-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate](#u-2026-11-15-engine-free-base-1-0-gate).
- [U-2026-11-08-semantics-x-is-profile-choice](#u-2026-11-08-semantics-x-is-profile-choice) (2026-11-08): semantics="X" is a choice of profile to verify under, not a frontend-only mode; the note claiming the reference is the only verification semantics is withdrawn.
- [U-2026-11-08-lowerings-lifts-symmetric](#u-2026-11-08-lowerings-lifts-symmetric) (2026-11-08): Lowerings and lifts stay separate machineries, each with one shape across every target engine or source frontend.
- [U-2026-11-08-engine-package-structure-first](#u-2026-11-08-engine-package-structure-first) (2026-11-08): Each engine is one directory with its Lean package, Python distribution, facts and tests, entering through the plugin interface; feature work pauses until the structure exists, wrong-answer bugs excepted. Amended by [U-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends](#u-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends).
- [U-2026-11-08-engine-behaviour-in-profiles](#u-2026-11-08-engine-behaviour-in-profiles) (2026-11-08): Engine-specific behaviour moves out of the core and the shared path into each engine's profile, dialect, writer or shims.
- [U-2026-11-07-reference-evaluator-conformance-only](#u-2026-11-07-reference-evaluator-conformance-only) (2026-11-07): The Lean reference evaluator serves conformance testing and possibly a Substrait oracle, never as an execution fallback for user plans.
- [U-2026-11-06-core-design-dossier](#u-2026-11-06-core-design-dossier) (2026-11-06): Core design decisions - typed Value as a tagged sum, D25 before 1.0, D26 two NULL equalities, D27 Rel.Small the only size assumption, D28 lexical lambdas, unordered FETCH refused in refresh unless deterministic, every core epic before 1.0.
- [D15](decisions.md#d15-where-the-observations-live) (2026-11-04): One choices table holds each option's class, observation q and shim per profile, so realizability rows come from one table.
- [D7](decisions.md#d7-per-plan-checks) (2026-11-04): Translators are proved once, entry by entry; single plans are checked only for translations sverify does not own.
- [D1](decisions.md#d1-where-the-tables-live) (2026-11-04): Lean holds the option and profile tables; Python mirrors them and a test compares the two.
- [U-2026-11-03-hub-architecture](#u-2026-11-03-hub-architecture) (2026-11-03): The architecture is a hub: a loose core with Substrait, the reference and each engine as refinements.
- [U-2026-11-02-lowering-generated-by-metaprogram](#u-2026-11-02-lowering-generated-by-metaprogram) (2026-11-02): Lowering entries are generated by a Lean metaprogram from the start; hand-written entries only where the search fails.
- [U-2026-11-02-isthmus-dropped](#u-2026-11-02-isthmus-dropped) (2026-11-02): Isthmus and Calcite are dropped as a SQL frontend, leaving SQLGlot and Ibis as the frontends.
- [U-2026-11-02-generated-lift-prioritized](#u-2026-11-02-generated-lift-prioritized) (2026-11-02): Generating the DuckDB SQL lift table from Lean (#268) is prioritized right after the SILENT/SATURATE step of M3; the lift then reads the generated table.
- [U-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis](#u-2026-11-01-frontends-sqlglot-ibis) (2026-11-01): Sverify's frontends are SQLGlot and Ibis, each with sverify's own Substrait producer; Isthmus and DataFusion's SQL planner are removed as frontends. Amended by [U-2026-11-05-datafusion-not-reference](#u-2026-11-05-datafusion-not-reference).

### CI and process

- [F172](#f172) (2026-11-20): Approves #1879 (the F3 no-double transfer) on reading once the lean job is green and doc edits R1 (changelog title names the three engines) and R2 (trusted-base kinds assumption) are in; F1 closes the gate's link later.
- [F156](#f156) (2026-11-19): #1838 meets ruling 151's M1 to M5 and merges once CI is green; ruling 151's refinement of 148 stands for the remaining move PRs.
- [F155](#f155) (2026-11-19): #1805 at b1509914 meets rulings 146 A1, 135 and 130 and is fine for the label; a follow-up gives one Nat-valued scaled tolerance definition per measure shared by measureEps and OpenSums.
- [F128](#f128) (2026-11-19): #1774 accepted as meeting ruling 107 (F1-F4); non-blocking follow-up: tests over open choices check membership (row count, sort keys) and `TIES_OPEN` is derived from the plan.
- [F125](#f125) (2026-11-19): Fable drafts the proven-optimizations epic body (labels epic, optimization, core, proofs); the user ruled that the coordinator posts it.
- [F93](#f93) (2026-11-19): #1712 (N14): ruling 88's lines 1, 2, 4, 5 are met; line 3 stays open because it was measured in a built-state run; one like-for-like run (L1, bound 1042/2545 MB) decides it. Addendum: Polars' later overflow raise is accepted with O1 (changelog) and O2 (failed write removes its directory).
- [F88](#f88) (2026-11-19): N14 builds now without first splitting hash table from scan/sink buffers; its acceptance replaces ruling 87's list: consolidation peak held at most 10% above #1660's refresh (947 MB at 2M, 2314 MB at 8M), plus lines for insert-only refreshes, deletes and the refresh-read loop.
- [U-2026-11-19-tests-in-ci](#u-2026-11-19-tests-in-ci) (2026-11-19): Tests run in CI after a push, and homelab is only for benchmarks, regens, long checker runs and check_generated builds.
- [U-2026-11-19-rule-by-core-algebra](#u-2026-11-19-rule-by-core-algebra) (2026-11-19): Rulings are judged against the core's algebraic structure, naming which structure a proposal changes and checking its laws hold.
- [U-2026-11-19-perf-through-each-pr](#u-2026-11-19-perf-through-each-pr) (2026-11-19): Performance comes through each PR's own improvement rather than dedicated performance sessions.
- [U-2026-11-19-optimizer-always-running](#u-2026-11-19-optimizer-always-running) (2026-11-19): A standing optimizer session works on proved optimizations continuously and never blocks other work.
- [U-2026-11-19-observables-hold-lifted](#u-2026-11-19-observables-hold-lifted) (2026-11-19): The hold is lifted: the observables design #1285 is assigned, so determinism and row-order exposure proceed through it.
- [F-1641-d46-ok](#f-1641-d46-ok) (2026-11-18): Post-merge review: #1641's D46 part stands as merged with all five changes in; nothing reverts. #1643 (D46 B) still merges only after step A2.
- [F78](#f78) (2026-11-18): Fable agrees with the coordinator's homelab-QA condition on #1631, with one line to confirm in the log: each 'The checker said:' in the 14 failures is the refusal its test expects.
- [U-2026-11-18-small-slowdowns-acceptable](#u-2026-11-18-small-slowdowns-acceptable) (2026-11-18): Small stated performance slowdowns are acceptable, but run and build times must not balloon, so trends across PRs are watched and fixed.
- [U-2026-11-18-self-hosted-runners-allowed](#u-2026-11-18-self-hosted-runners-allowed) (2026-11-18): Ephemeral AWS spot self-hosted runners are allowed for CI, while homelab stays a driver-run box and not a runner.
- [U-2026-11-18-aws-runners-paid-plan](#u-2026-11-18-aws-runners-paid-plan) (2026-11-18): The AWS runners move to the Paid plan with strict budget controls: a $100 monthly cap, a $15 daily budget and a scale-up deny at 80 %, and caps or pool sizes change only with the user.
- [U-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages](#u-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages) (2026-11-16): engine-profiles.md, float-bounds.md, optimizations.md and proved-theorems.md are also reference pages.
- [U-2026-11-16-coordinator-adds-labels](#u-2026-11-16-coordinator-adds-labels) (2026-11-16): The coordinator may add the reviewed label after a reviewer's approval, so the auto-queue merges the PR once qa is green.
- [U-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends](#u-2026-11-15-structure-freeze-ends) (2026-11-15): The structure-first feature freeze ends with #1189's completion.
- [U-2026-11-15-reference-pages-lean-names](#u-2026-11-15-reference-pages-lean-names) (2026-11-15): Lean names stay only on reference pages (trusted-base.md, reference-semantics.md); other user pages name no Lean lemma, D-number or issue number. Amended by [U-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages](#u-2026-11-16-four-more-reference-pages).
- [U-2026-11-15-autonomous-worker-pool](#u-2026-11-15-autonomous-worker-pool) (2026-11-15): The coordinator continues without the user, with Sonnet workers implementing and Fable advising on architecture.
- [U-2026-11-09-standard-drivers](#u-2026-11-09-standard-drivers) (2026-11-09): Expensive runs go through standardised drivers that hold capacity slots rather than hand-written wait loops.
- [U-2026-11-09-perf-checks-report-only](#u-2026-11-09-perf-checks-report-only) (2026-11-09): The Lean budget and checker-speed checks only report and never block important work; a PR over budget explains it and a follow-up fixes it.
- [U-2026-11-09-parallel-lean-builds](#u-2026-11-09-parallel-lean-builds) (2026-11-09): More than one Lean build may run at a time on the laptop and homelab, within the slots and the load limits.
- [U-2026-11-09-homelab-home-clean](#u-2026-11-09-homelab-home-clean) (2026-11-09): Homelab scratch goes in a run directory or a per-session results folder and is removed when the task ends.
- [U-2026-11-09-fast-builds-priority](#u-2026-11-09-fast-builds-priority) (2026-11-09): Build optimizations are a standing priority because they set development velocity. Amended by [U-2026-11-09-perf-checks-report-only](#u-2026-11-09-perf-checks-report-only).
- [U-2026-11-08-pr-two-improvements](#u-2026-11-08-pr-two-improvements) (2026-11-08): Every pull request brings two measured improvements beside its main change, one to performance and one to code quality.
- [U-2026-11-08-only-1-0-issues](#u-2026-11-08-only-1-0-issues) (2026-11-08): Work picks up ready issues only in the 1.0 milestones.
- [U-2026-11-08-fable-signs-off-d-numbers](#u-2026-11-08-fable-signs-off-d-numbers) (2026-11-08): Fable's approval suffices for D-numbers unless the user says otherwise, starting with D35.
- [U-2026-11-08-fable-owns-architecture](#u-2026-11-08-fable-owns-architecture) (2026-11-08): Fable owns architecture, design reviews and D-number sign-offs, and its answer is final for the coordinator.
- [U-2026-11-08-fable-escalates-to-user](#u-2026-11-08-fable-escalates-to-user) (2026-11-08): Fable, not the coordinator, takes changes to the reference's promises and irreversible or outward-facing actions to the user.
- [U-2026-11-08-coordinator-owns-scheduling](#u-2026-11-08-coordinator-owns-scheduling) (2026-11-08): Scheduling, ordering, queueing and re-plans are the coordinator's own call, with Fable consulted before the user.
- [U-2026-11-08-batch-size-one-line](#u-2026-11-08-batch-size-one-line) (2026-11-08): The batch merger takes one queue line holding every PR that is or soon will be green, rather than one line per arrival.
- [U-2026-11-07-squash-snowflake-prs](#u-2026-11-07-squash-snowflake-prs) (2026-11-07): Snowflake PRs are checked for private details before they queue, and squash-merged.
- [U-2026-11-05-no-sf-upstream-without-maintainer](#u-2026-11-05-no-sf-upstream-without-maintainer) (2026-11-05): Snowflake bugs are not filed upstream without the maintainer.
- [U-2026-11-05-coordinator-drives-roadmap](#u-2026-11-05-coordinator-drives-roadmap) (2026-11-05): The coordinator drives the 1.0 roadmap independently and batches questions for the user.
- [U-2026-11-04-spec-gaps-on-ledger](#u-2026-11-04-spec-gaps-on-ledger) (2026-11-04): Behaviour the Substrait YAMLs leave unspecified but could express is a spec gap, recorded as an ST-<n> document of kind spec-gap in the PR that meets it.
- [U-2026-11-04-reduced-ci-runs](#u-2026-11-04-reduced-ci-runs) (2026-11-04): CI runs reduced job sets on pull requests according to what a change can affect, as CLAUDE.md records.
- [U-2026-11-04-larger-prs-not-stacks](#u-2026-11-04-larger-prs-not-stacks) (2026-11-04): Items touching the same files go to one session as one larger PR rather than a stack, and only disjoint files run in parallel.
- [U-2026-11-04-full-hypothesis-counts](#u-2026-11-04-full-hypothesis-counts) (2026-11-04): CI keeps the full Hypothesis example counts.
- [U-2026-11-04-bugs-optimizations-coverage](#u-2026-11-04-bugs-optimizations-coverage) (2026-11-04): With no task assigned, work picks bugs, then optimizations, then coverage, on non-overlapping files.
- [U-2026-11-02-ready-prs-not-drafts](#u-2026-11-02-ready-prs-not-drafts) (2026-11-02): Sessions open PRs ready for review rather than as drafts, unless the work is unfinished.
- [U-2026-11-02-no-main-updates-before-merge](#u-2026-11-02-no-main-updates-before-merge) (2026-11-02): PRs are not updated with main before merging, only when they conflict or fail CI.
